<h3><a href="https://x.com/KEI_Ediforce/status/2106209244446101963">Testing Clef-flash as a local Jev on Mac</a></h3>
<p>The author ran Cloudflare&#39;s judgment model Clef-flash on an M4 Max Mac to see if it could serve as a locally running Jev. All 16 custom routing queries matched, but each took about 0.6–1.5 seconds, images 4–9 seconds, and MLX acceleration was also tested.</p>

<p>A user tested MetaCog + Jev on Celeris_ai and found it not very useful for this use case. Adding Jev as a judge to Celeris-1 raised AIME from 23% to 53% (+30%) and MATH-500 by 4.6 points, at the cost of ~3.5x latency and $0.04 per run, with no benefit on multiple-choice questions.</p>

<h3><a href="https://x.com/0xAIlabs/status/2105633417702346754">Jev vs GPT-6 Luna: Higher Score Isn&#39;t the Part That Makes an Agent Safe</a></h3>
<p>A tester ran the same 113 decisions through Jev and GPT-6 Luna. Luna got more right (81% vs 67%), and with more thinking reached 99%. When only high-confidence answers were kept, Jev&#39;s accuracy jumped notably, sparking discussion about safety and confidence mechanisms.</p>
<p><strong>中文摘要：</strong>有测试者将同一组113个决策分别交给Jev和GPT-6 Luna处理，Luna正确率更高（81%对比67%），但要求思考后达到99%。随后只保留两个模型高置信度的答案，Jev的准确率出现大幅提升，引发对模型安全性和置信度机制的讨论。</p>

<h3><a href="https://x.com/ericmacdougall/status/2105312459271680183">Jev Choice Confidence: Limitations and Advice</a></h3>
<p>Jev Choice confidence summarizes option distribution, not observed correctness. A single threshold may hide date, missing-state, or adversarial misses. Label outcomes independently by slice and consequence, and use that evidence to set escalation.</p>
<p><strong>中文摘要：</strong>Jev Choice置信度总结的是选项分布而非实际正确性。单一阈值可能掩盖日期、缺失状态或对抗性错误，建议按切片和结果独立标记，并据此设置升级。</p>
<p><strong>Eric MacDougall</strong> · @ericmacdougall · 2026-09-30T15:02:59.000Z</p>
<p>💬 0 &nbsp; 🔁 0 &nbsp; ♡ 0 &nbsp; 📊 63</p>

<h3><a href="https://x.com/doubleemt/status/2104683332152029342">Jev for Clustering and Knowledge Graph Construction: Experience Report</a></h3>
<p>The post notes that Jev tends to create new groups rather than adding to existing ones in clustering, but shows no deterioration in decision-making with higher choice cardinality, unlike early LLM agents.</p>
<p><strong>中文摘要：</strong>帖子指出 Jev 在聚类任务中倾向于创建新组而非加入现有组，但在高基数选项下决策能力没有退化，与早期 LLM 代理形成对比。</p>

<h3><a href="https://x.com/tk_ai_create/status/2103356322205106343">Pre-deployment checks for the Jev judgment AI</a></h3>
<p>TK shares 5 pre-deployment steps for Jev, an AI that doesn&#39;t write articles but makes judgments and returns fixed formats like Choice, Score, Yes/No: test with 20 right-answer samples, narrow judgments to one, compare probability with the correct answer, route low-confidence results to humans, and never delegate sending, deletion, or billing to it—emphasizing escape routes first.</p>
<p><strong>中文摘要：</strong>TK分享让不写文章、只做判断的AI“Jev”以Choice、Score、Yes/No等固定格式输出时，导入前应执行的5个步骤：用带正确答案的20个样本测试、把判断收敛为1个、比对概率与正确答案、低置信度退回人工、不让它负责发送/删除/扣费等操作，强调先留好退路。</p>

<h3><a href="https://x.com/TanzilTaqi/status/2103426512494162115">Using Jev to Verify App Reviews</a></h3>
<p>In the example, after rewriting the review as a verifiable statement, Jev gives 98% true. Tip: write it as a statement that can be true or false, not &quot;what&#39;s wrong?&quot;</p>
<p><strong>中文摘要：</strong>示例中，将评论改写成可验证陈述后，Jev给出98%真实性判断。提示：把问题写成可判断真伪的陈述句，而非“有什么问题？”。</p>

<h3><a href="https://x.com/StevenDenney86/status/2103017960130461764">Jev Benchmarked: 50k Passages in 37 Minutes, 9x Faster than Qwen</a></h3>
<p>Developer Steven Denney shares: encoding ~50,000 passages from a North Korean economics journal corpus with Jev and Qwen. Jev finished in 37 minutes for $4.46, 9× faster than Qwen. When Claude Sonnet adjudicated a random sample plus cases with highest model disagreement, it sided mainly with Jev.</p>

<h3><a href="https://x.com/sreerajta94/status/2103005813086490806">JEV as a Semantic Fitness Function: Evolving a Mug into a Box</a></h3>
<p>The author starts with a mug as the seed and asks JEV to turn it into a box. No geometry is generated by an LLM; JEV is used only as the semantic fitness function. Evolutionary search proposes mutations, and JEV judges which candidates are closer to a &#39;box&#39;.</p>

<h3><a href="https://x.com/pabloramosuy/status/2102738022148039107">Experiment Training an S1-Type Jev Model with 22K Real Decisions</a></h3>
<p>Pablo Ramos shares an experiment: trained an S1-type Jev model with 22,098 real decisions from Uruguayan workshops. Two of the six questions he asked didn&#39;t measure anything, which turned out to be the important part of the experiment.</p>

<h3><a href="https://x.com/MajdAICode/status/2102755882333294594">Jev in Agent Workflows: A Layered Architecture View</a></h3>
<p>The author proposes splitting the agent execution environment into three layers: DigitalOcean runs the agents, Orca/Herdr handles parallel distribution and monitoring, and Jev handles micro-decisions in the workflow. Jev decides who receives a task, whether deep review is needed, and whether to run PHPUnit or E2E tests. The core point is that value comes not just from running 5 agents in the cloud, but from how these decisions are coordinated.</p>
<p><strong>中文摘要：</strong>作者提出将Agent执行环境分为三层：DigitalOcean负责运行Agents，Orca/Herdr负责并行分发与监控，Jev负责工作流中的微观决策。Jev决定任务接收者、是否需要Deep Review，以及选择PHPUnit还是E2E测试。核心观点是价值不仅在于云端运行多个Agents，更在于这些决策的协调。</p>
